Create `tools/eval/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/eval/command/Session.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.eval.command

import dev.kestrel.eval.config.LocalConfig
import dev.kestrel.eval.config.Redactions
import dev.kestrel.eval.device.Device
import dev.kestrel.eval.device.DeviceGuard
import dev.kestrel.eval.device.DeviceKind
import dev.kestrel.eval.hook.HookClient
import dev.kestrel.eval.hook.Hooks
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.AccountGuard
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.Console
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.Installer
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.LineWriter
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.LiveGate
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.LiveSession
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.Preflight
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.PrintConsole
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.Restores
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.RunOptions
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.Runner
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Task
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.TaskKind
import java.nio.file.Path

/**
 * One command's device and what works on it (design D2 and D3 of sub-project 6): the local configuration and the
 * console's redactions, the restores, the device that [DeviceGuard] accepted (a refusal otherwise), its hooks, the
 * preflight and the runner. [guarded] runs a command's body between the reads of kestrel-api37's accounts and profile,
 * and puts the device back at its end, however it ends.
 */
class Session(private val environment: Environment, serial: String) {
    val config: LocalConfig = LocalConfig.read(environment.root.resolve("corpus").resolve("owner").resolve("a71-config.json"))
    val console: Console = PrintConsole(environment.out, Redactions(config))
    val restores = Restores(console, Redactions(config), environment.addShutdownHook)
    val device: Device = environment.adb(serial).let { adb -> Device(adb, DeviceGuard(environment.clock).accept(adb), environment.clock) }
    val hooks = Hooks(HookClient(device))
    val work: Path = environment.root.resolve("build").resolve("eval").resolve(serial)
    val preflight = Preflight(device, hooks, restores, environment.clock, Installer(environment.root, environment.processes))

    /** Before the first run: a live session left on goes off without `--live`, the preflight, and with `--live` the live gate. */
    fun prepare(tasks: List<Task>, live: Boolean) {
        if (!live) LiveSession(hooks, console, device.serial).ensureOff()
        preflight.prepare(needsTestApp = tasks.any { it.kind == TaskKind.STAND_IN || it.kind == TaskKind.INJECTION })
        if (live) LiveGate(hooks, restores, device.serial).open()
    }

    fun runner(options: RunOptions, writer: LineWriter): Runner =
        Runner(device, hooks, restores, environment.clock, console, config, work, writer, options, preflight::waitListening)

    fun <T> guarded(body: () -> T): T {
        preflight.wake()
        val accounts = if (device.kind == DeviceKind.EMULATOR) AccountGuard(device, hooks) else null
        val before = accounts?.read()
        try {
            return body()
        } finally {
            restores.report(restores.runAll())
            if (accounts != null && before != null) accounts.check(before)
        }
    }
}
```

Create `tools/eval/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/eval/command/RunCommand.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.eval.command

import dev.kestrel.eval.device.Refusal
import dev.kestrel.eval.report.ResultsFile
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.CheckResult
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.LineWriter
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.Reason
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.RunLine
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.RunOptions
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.RunResult
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.ScriptInstaller
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Catalogue
import java.nio.file.Paths

/**
 * `run --device <s> --task <name>` (design D2 of sub-project 6): one task, as task-check.ps1 ran it, with its lines on the
 * console and appended to `--results` with the step `task`. Without `--live` the runs use the task's form without a
 * model: its stand-in with its script, as scripts\a71-agent.ps1's dry run needs, and `--dry-run` runs that form once; a
 * task without one writes `result=skipped reason=untestable`. Returns 0 when no run failed, else 1.
 */
class RunCommand(private val environment: Environment, private val command: Command.Run) {
    fun execute(lines: (LineWriter) -> Unit): Int {
        val task = Catalogue.find(command.task) ?: throw Refusal("Unknown task ${command.task}; scripts\\eval.ps1 list shows the tasks.")
        val session = Session(environment, command.device)
        val writer = LineWriter(session.console, command.results?.let { ResultsFile(Paths.get(it), environment.clock) })
        lines(writer)
        val dryRun = task.dryRun
        if (!command.live && dryRun == null) {
            writer.write(RunLine(task.name, 1, RunResult.SKIPPED, null, CheckResult.SKIPPED, Reason.UNTESTABLE))
            session.console.line("Task ${task.name} has no form without a model, so it runs only with --live.")
            return 0
        }
        val executed = if (command.live) task else dryRun!!.standIn?.let { Catalogue.find(it) } ?: task
        val script = if (command.live) null else ScriptInstaller.resource(dryRun!!.script)
        val runs = if (command.dryRun) 1 else command.runs
        return session.guarded {
            session.prepare(listOf(executed), command.live)
            val failures = session.runner(RunOptions(command.live, command.model, command.name), writer).runAll(executed, script, runs)
            if (failures > 0) 1 else 0
        }
    }
}
```

Create `tools/eval/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/eval/command/SeriesCommand.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.eval.command

import dev.kestrel.eval.device.DeviceKind
import dev.kestrel.eval.device.Refusal
import dev.kestrel.eval.report.Report
import dev.kestrel.eval.report.ResultsFile
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.LineWriter
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.RunOptions
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.ScriptInstaller
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Catalogue
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.InjectionFixtures
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Task
import java.nio.file.Path
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import kotlin.time.Duration.Companion.seconds

/**
 * A series of tasks into a new results folder `eval-results\<device>[-injection]-<yyyyMMdd-HHmmss>\` (design D6 and D10
 * of sub-project 6): `runs.txt` in `Add-SessionResult`'s form, with the line `<task> on <the A71|kestrel-api37>, <n> runs`
 * before each task's run lines, which a71-agent.ps1's `Get-SittingRuns` and `ConvertFrom-TaskLine` read, and
 * `report.json`. `suite` runs the suite of the device (3 runs each by default); `injection` the 14 fixtures on
 * kestrel-api37 (once without a model, 5 times live, by default). Returns 0 when no run failed, else 1.
 */
class SeriesCommand(
    private val environment: Environment,
    private val name: String,
    private val live: Boolean,
    private val runs: Int,
    private val pick: (DeviceKind) -> List<Task>,
) {
    fun execute(serial: String, lines: (LineWriter) -> Unit): Int {
        val session = Session(environment, serial)
        val kind = session.device.kind
        val tasks = pick(kind)
        if (tasks.isEmpty()) throw Refusal("The command $name has no task for ${kind.label}.")
        val stamp = environment.clock.now().format(STAMP)
        val folder: Path = environment.root.resolve("eval-results").resolve("${kind.folder}${if (name == "injection") "-injection" else ""}-$stamp")
        val runsFile = ResultsFile(folder.resolve("runs.txt"), environment.clock)
        val writer = LineWriter(session.console, runsFile)
        lines(writer)
        val start = environment.clock.now()
        return session.guarded {
            session.prepare(tasks, live)
            val runner = session.runner(RunOptions(live), writer)
            var failures = 0
            for (task in tasks) {
                val count = task.runsAtMost?.let { minOf(it, runs) } ?: runs
                runsFile.append(name, "${task.name} on ${kind.label}, $count runs")
                failures += runner.runAll(task, if (live) null else ScriptInstaller.resource(task.dryRun!!.script), runs)
            }
            Report.of(kind, commit(), live, start, environment.clock.now(), writer.lines).write(folder.resolve("report.json"))
            session.console.line("The results are in $folder.")
            if (failures > 0) 1 else 0
        }
    }

    private fun commit(): String = try {
        environment.processes.run(listOf("git", "-C", environment.root.toString(), "rev-parse", "HEAD"), 30.seconds).out.trim().ifEmpty { "unknown" }
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        "unknown"
    }

    companion object {
        private val STAMP = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss")

        fun suite(environment: Environment, command: Command.Suite): SeriesCommand =
            SeriesCommand(environment, "suite", command.live, command.runs) { kind ->
                val suite = Catalogue.suite(kind, command.live)
                val unknown = command.tasks.orEmpty().filter { name -> suite.none { it.name == name } }
                if (unknown.isNotEmpty()) throw Refusal("The suite of ${kind.label} has no task ${unknown.joinToString()}.")
                command.tasks?.let { names -> suite.filter { it.name in names } } ?: suite
            }

        fun injection(environment: Environment, command: Command.Injection): SeriesCommand =
            SeriesCommand(environment, "injection", command.live, command.runs ?: if (command.live) 5 else 1) { kind ->
                if (kind != DeviceKind.EMULATOR) throw Refusal("The injection fixtures run on kestrel-api37 only.")
                InjectionFixtures.ALL
            }
    }
}
```

Create `tools/eval/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/eval/command/RehearseCommand.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.eval.command

import dev.kestrel.eval.device.DeviceChanged
import dev.kestrel.eval.device.Refusal
import dev.kestrel.eval.hook.HookAnswer
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.Markers
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Catalogue
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Rehearsal
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.RunScope
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.RunValues
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Setup
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.SetupResult
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Task

/**
 * `rehearse --device <s>` (design D7 of sub-project 6): each task's setup, the end state that its rehearsal makes by adb
 * or by the script's own taps, its checks, which must pass, and its restores, and then the values that its steps
 * changed, compared with their values before; without Kestrel's task or a model, so it runs on the A71 too. A task whose
 * check needs Kestrel's task rehearses its setup and restore only. Prints one line per task:
 * `rehearse=<task> result=<pass|fail|skipped> reason=<ok|setup-only|untestable|setup|check|restore|changed>`.
 */
class RehearseCommand(private val environment: Environment, private val command: Command.Rehearse) {
    fun execute(): Int {
        val session = Session(environment, command.device)
        val kind = session.device.kind
        val tasks = Catalogue.ALL.filter { kind in it.devices }
        val unknown = command.tasks.orEmpty().filter { name -> tasks.none { it.name == name } }
        if (unknown.isNotEmpty()) throw Refusal("${kind.label} rehearses no task ${unknown.joinToString()}.")
        val chosen = command.tasks?.let { names -> tasks.filter { it.name in names } } ?: tasks
        return session.guarded {
            session.prepare(chosen, live = false)
            var failed = 0
            for (task in chosen) {
                val (result, reason) = rehearse(session, task)
                if (result == "fail") failed++
                session.console.line("rehearse=${task.name} result=$result reason=$reason")
            }
            if (failed > 0) 1 else 0
        }
    }

    private fun rehearse(session: Session, task: Task): Pair<String, String> {
        val scope = RunScope(
            session.device, session.hooks, session.restores, environment.clock, session.console, session.config, session.work,
            RunValues(Markers().next(emptyList())),
        )
        val mark = session.restores.mark()
        val before = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
        val probed = mutableListOf<Setup>()
        var outcome = "pass" to "ok"
        try {
            for (step in task.setup + (task.rehearsal as? Rehearsal.Steps)?.steps.orEmpty()) {
                before.putAll(step.probe(scope))
                probed.add(step)
                if (step.apply(scope) is SetupResult.Untestable) {
                    outcome = "skipped" to "untestable"
                    break
                }
            }
            if (outcome.first == "pass") {
                outcome = when (task.rehearsal) {
                    is Rehearsal.SetupOnly -> "pass" to "setup-only"
                    is Rehearsal.Steps -> if (task.check.all { it.passes(scope, HookAnswer.EMPTY) }) "pass" to "ok" else "fail" to "check"
                }
            }
        } catch (e: DeviceChanged) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            session.console.warn("Rehearsal of ${task.name}: ${e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName}")
            outcome = "fail" to "setup"
        }
        val failures = session.restores.runDownTo(mark)
        session.restores.report(failures)
        if (failures.isNotEmpty()) return "fail" to "restore"
        val after = probed.fold(mutableMapOf<String, String>()) { values, step -> values.apply { putAll(step.probe(scope)) } }
        val changed = before.keys.filter { before[it] != after[it] }
        if (changed.isNotEmpty()) {
            session.console.warn("Rehearsal of ${task.name}: not as before: ${changed.joinToString()}")
            return "fail" to "changed"
        }
        return outcome
    }
}
```

Create `tools/eval/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/eval/command/ListCommand.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.eval.command

import dev.kestrel.eval.device.DeviceGuard
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Catalogue
import dev.kestrel.eval.task.Rehearsal
import java.io.PrintStream

/**
 * `list [--device <s>]`: every task with its kind, its devices, its form without a model and its rehearsal; with a
 * device, only the tasks that it runs.
 */
class ListCommand(private val environment: Environment, private val command: Command.ListTasks) {
    fun execute(out: PrintStream): Int {
        val kind = command.device?.let { DeviceGuard(environment.clock).accept(environment.adb(it)).kind }
        for (task in Catalogue.ALL.filter { kind == null || kind in it.devices }) {
            val dryRun = task.dryRun?.let { dry -> dry.script + (dry.standIn?.let { " on $it" } ?: "") } ?: "none"
            val rehearsal = if (task.rehearsal is Rehearsal.Steps) "steps" else "setup-only"
            out.println(
                "task=${task.name} kind=${task.kind.name.lowercase().replace('_', '-')} devices=${task.devices.sorted().joinToString(",") { it.folder }} " +
                    "dryRun=${dryRun.replace(' ', '_')} rehearsal=$rehearsal",
            )
        }
        return 0
    }
}
```

Create `tools/eval/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/eval/report/Report.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.eval.report

import dev.kestrel.eval.device.DeviceKind
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.RunLine
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.RunResult
import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import java.nio.file.Files
import java.nio.file.Path
import java.time.LocalDateTime

@Serializable
data class Tokens(val input: Long = 0, val output: Long = 0, val cacheRead: Long = 0, val cacheWrite: Long = 0) {
    operator fun plus(other: Tokens) = Tokens(input + other.input, output + other.output, cacheRead + other.cacheRead, cacheWrite + other.cacheWrite)
}

/**
 * A task's part of a report (design D10 of sub-project 6): the passes of the runs that ran, the runs that ran, the skipped
 * runs by their reason, the mean steps and working time of the runs that ran, the AI Credits with the number of runs whose
 * credits are unknown, and the tokens.
 */
@Serializable
data class TaskReport(
    val task: String,
    val passes: Int,
    val runs: Int,
    val skipped: Map<String, Int>,
    val meanSteps: Double,
    val meanWorkMs: Double,
    val credits: Double,
    val unknownCredits: Int,
    val tokens: Tokens,
)

@Serializable
data class Totals(val passes: Int, val runs: Int, val skipped: Int, val credits: Double, val unknownCredits: Int, val tokens: Tokens)

/**
 * `report.json` of a results folder (design D10 of sub-project 6), the per-task fields of plan 6A; plan 6B adds the
 * egress check's result and plan 6C datamarking's state.
 */
@Serializable
data class Report(
    val device: String,
    val commit: String,
    val model: String,
    val live: Boolean,
    val start: String,
    val end: String,
    val tasks: List<TaskReport>,
    val totals: Totals,
) {
    fun write(file: Path) {
        Files.createDirectories(file.toAbsolutePath().parent)
        Files.writeString(file, JSON.encodeToString(serializer(), this) + "\n")
    }

    companion object {
        private val JSON = Json { prettyPrint = true }

        /** The report of [lines], in the order of their tasks; a run that was skipped counts only among the skipped. */
        fun of(device: DeviceKind, commit: String, live: Boolean, start: LocalDateTime, end: LocalDateTime, lines: List<RunLine>): Report {
            val tasks = lines.groupBy { it.task }.map { (task, runs) -> task(task, runs) }
            val model = lines.mapNotNull { it.answer?.get("model") }.firstOrNull { it != "none" } ?: "none"
            val totals = Totals(
                tasks.sumOf { it.passes }, tasks.sumOf { it.runs }, tasks.sumOf { it.skipped.values.sum() }, tasks.sumOf { it.credits },
                tasks.sumOf { it.unknownCredits }, tasks.fold(Tokens()) { sum, task -> sum + task.tokens },
            )
            return Report(device.folder, commit, model, live, start.toString(), end.toString(), tasks, totals)
        }

        private fun task(task: String, lines: List<RunLine>): TaskReport {
            val ran = lines.filter { it.result != RunResult.SKIPPED }
            fun number(line: RunLine, field: String) = line.answer?.get(field)?.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
            val credits = lines.map { it.credits ?: it.answer?.get("credits") ?: "0.00" }
            return TaskReport(
                task = task,
                passes = ran.count { it.result == RunResult.PASS },
                runs = ran.size,
                skipped = lines.filter { it.result == RunResult.SKIPPED }.groupingBy { it.reason.wire }.eachCount(),
                meanSteps = if (ran.isEmpty()) 0.0 else ran.map { number(it, "steps") }.average(),
                meanWorkMs = if (ran.isEmpty()) 0.0 else ran.map { number(it, "workMs") }.average(),
                credits = credits.mapNotNull { it.toDoubleOrNull() }.sum(),
                unknownCredits = credits.count { it.toDoubleOrNull() == null },
                tokens = lines.fold(Tokens()) { sum, line ->
                    sum + Tokens(number(line, "in"), number(line, "out"), number(line, "cacheRead"), number(line, "cacheWrite"))
                },
            )
        }
    }
}
```

Replace the whole `tools/eval/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/eval/Cli.kt` with:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.eval

import dev.kestrel.eval.command.Arguments
import dev.kestrel.eval.command.Command
import dev.kestrel.eval.command.Environment
import dev.kestrel.eval.command.ListCommand
import dev.kestrel.eval.command.RehearseCommand
import dev.kestrel.eval.command.RunCommand
import dev.kestrel.eval.command.SeriesCommand
import dev.kestrel.eval.command.UsageError
import dev.kestrel.eval.config.LocalConfig
import dev.kestrel.eval.config.Redactions
import dev.kestrel.eval.device.Refusal
import dev.kestrel.eval.run.LineWriter

/**
 * The command line of :tools:eval (design D2 of sub-project 6) and its exit codes: 0 when every run passed or was skipped
 * with a reason, 1 when a run failed or an error stopped the runs, and 2 for a refusal before any run (a device, the live
 * gate, a missing configuration, a command line that it does not take), which starts no run and writes no run line.
 */
class Cli(private val environment: Environment) {
    fun run(args: List<String>): Int {
        val out = environment.out
        val command = try {
            Arguments.parse(args)
        } catch (e: UsageError) {
            out.println(e.message)
            out.println(USAGE)
            return EXIT_REFUSED
        }
        var writer: LineWriter? = null
        val keep: (LineWriter) -> Unit = { writer = it }
        return try {
            when (command) {
                is Command.Run -> RunCommand(environment, command).execute(keep)
                is Command.Suite -> SeriesCommand.suite(environment, command).execute(command.device, keep)
                is Command.Injection -> SeriesCommand.injection(environment, command).execute(command.device, keep)
                is Command.Rehearse -> RehearseCommand(environment, command).execute()
                is Command.ListTasks -> ListCommand(environment, command).execute(out)
            }
        } catch (e: Refusal) {
            out.println("REFUSED: ${redactions().forConsole(e.message.orEmpty())}")
            if (writer?.lines.isNullOrEmpty()) EXIT_REFUSED else EXIT_FAILED
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            out.println("ERROR: ${redactions().forConsole(e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)}")
            EXIT_FAILED
        }
    }

    private fun redactions() = Redactions(LocalConfig.read(environment.root.resolve("corpus").resolve("owner").resolve("a71-config.json")))

    companion object {
        const val EXIT_PASSED = 0
        const val EXIT_FAILED = 1
        const val EXIT_REFUSED = 2

        val USAGE: String = """
            |Usage: scripts\eval.ps1 <command> [options]
            |  run --device <serial> --task <name> [--runs n] [--live] [--model id] [--name first-name] [--dry-run] [--results file]
            |  suite --device <serial> [--live] [--runs n] [--tasks a,b]
            |  injection --device <serial> [--live] [--runs n]
            |  rehearse --device <serial> [--tasks a,b]
            |  list [--device <serial>]
        """.trimMargin()
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.eval

import dev.kestrel.eval.command.Environment
import kotlin.system.exitProcess

/** Starts :tools:eval with the arguments that `scripts\eval.ps1` passes on, and ends with the command's exit code. */
fun main(args: Array<String>) {
    exitProcess(Cli(Environment.local(System.out)).run(args.toList()))
}
```

### Task 10: scripts\a71-agent.ps1 runs its tasks through the tool

**Files:**
- Modify: `scripts/a71-agent.ps1` (the header, the comments that name task-check.ps1, `Get-StandInTask` removed, `Invoke-TaskCheck` replaced by `Invoke-EvalTool` and `Invoke-Eval`, `Invoke-TaskRuns`, the e-mail runs without `Mail`, `waze-home` without its own test location, `KESTREL_AGENT_SESSION` removed), `scripts/tests/A71Agent.Tests.ps1`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's `scripts\eval.ps1 run --device <s> --task <name> --runs <n> --results <file> (--live | --dry-run) [--model id] [--name first-name]` with exit codes 0, 1 and 2, and its run lines in `Add-SessionResult`'s form; Task 1's `scripts\eval.ps1`.
- Produces: in `scripts\a71-agent.ps1`, `Invoke-EvalTool([string[]]$Arguments)` (runs `scripts\eval.ps1` and returns its exit code; the tests mock it) and `Invoke-Eval([string]$Device, [string]$Task, [int]$Runs, [hashtable]$With)`, which throws on exit code 2 and when a call left no run line; `Invoke-TaskRuns` keeps its parameters and its results (`<task> on <device>, <n> runs` first, the lines read back, the credits and the ceiling); `Get-StandInTask` is gone, and a sitting no longer sets `KESTREL_AGENT_SESSION`.

Design D16's move, callers first: `Invoke-TaskCheck` becomes `Invoke-Eval`, which runs the tool with `--live` in a real sitting 2 and with `--dry-run` in a dry run (the tool picks the stand-in and its script); `--model` and `--name` come from `$With`, and the tool reads the owner's addresses itself, so the e-mail runs no longer pass `Mail`. A refusal of the tool and a call without run lines throw, and `Invoke-TaskRuns` records them as it recorded task-check.ps1's errors, with the error's class only. Exit code 1 is no error. Sitting 2 still opens and closes the live sessions itself; the tool's own opening is then a no-op whose restore leaves the session open for the sitting to close. The tool's `waze-home` sets the A71's test location itself, so the sitting no longer wraps it in `Start-TestLocation`.

Counts: Pester N + 0 in each shell (`A71Agent.Tests.ps1` loses the test of `Get-StandInTask` and gains the test of `Invoke-Eval`).

```powershell
# The task of scripts\task-check.ps1 that a dry run runs instead of a live task, with its script; $null when the live
# task has no stand-in, as Waze.
function Get-StandInTask([string]$Task) {
    $map = @{
        'telegram-late' = 'stand-in-messenger-late'; 'telegram-photos' = 'stand-in-messenger-photos'; 'read-email' = 'stand-in-read'
        'hotspot' = 'stand-in-hotspot'; 'firefox-wiki' = 'stand-in-firefox'
    }
    if ($Task -match '^inject-\d{2}$') { return [pscustomobject]@{ Task = $Task; Script = 'tasks\hostile.json' } }
    if (-not $map.ContainsKey($Task)) { return $null }
    [pscustomobject]@{ Task = $map[$Task]; Script = "tasks\$($map[$Task]).json" }
}

# A number with at most $Digits decimals, in the invariant culture, for the results.
```

In `scripts/a71-agent.ps1`, replace:

```powershell
# Runs scripts\task-check.ps1 with the arguments, its output on the console.
function Invoke-TaskCheck([hashtable]$Arguments) { & "$PSScriptRoot\task-check.ps1" @Arguments | Out-Host }

# Runs scripts\task-check.ps1 for a task on a device: -Live in a real sitting, or the task's stand-in with its scripted
# model in a dry run (one run), so that no request reaches a provider. The runs' lines go into this sitting's results
# file, from which they are read back; their AI Credits are added, and the sitting stops at its ceiling, which is checked
# before the runs too (Assert-CreditsLeft). Returns the runs' lines as tables.
function Invoke-TaskRuns([string]$StepName, [string]$Device, [string]$Task, [int]$Runs = 3, [hashtable]$With = @{}) {
    Assert-CreditsLeft
    $arguments = @{ Serial = $Device; Task = $Task; Runs = $Runs; Results = $resultsFile } + $With
    if ($DryRun) {
        $standIn = Get-StandInTask $Task
        if (-not $standIn) {
            Add-Result $StepName "$Task on $(Get-DeviceName $Device): skipped in a dry run, because it has no stand-in"
            return @()
        }
        $arguments.Task = $standIn.Task
        $arguments.Script = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot $standIn.Script
        $arguments.Runs = 1
        $arguments.Remove('Mail')
    } else {
        $arguments.Live = $true
    }
    Add-Result $StepName "$($arguments.Task) on $(Get-DeviceName $Device), $($arguments.Runs) runs"
    $before = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $resultsFile).Count
    # A task whose setup or check fails is recorded, and the step goes on with its next task (design D27). Its error can
    # hold the owner's address, as read-email's mailto: command does, so only its class is written (task-5c-10-review M1).
    try {
        Invoke-TaskCheck $arguments
    } catch {
        Add-Result $StepName "$($arguments.Task) on $(Get-DeviceName $Device): failed: task-check.ps1 stopped with $($_.Exception.GetType().Name) before its runs ended"
    }
```

with:

```powershell
# Runs scripts\eval.ps1 with the arguments, its output on the console, and returns its exit code. A seam for the tests.
function Invoke-EvalTool([string[]]$Arguments) {
    & "$PSScriptRoot\eval.ps1" @Arguments | Out-Host
    $LASTEXITCODE
}

# Runs $Runs runs of a task on a device through :tools:eval (scripts\eval.ps1 run; design D16 of sub-project 6): --live in
# a real sitting, or the task's form without a model (--dry-run, its stand-in with its script, one run) in a dry run, so
# that no request reaches a provider; -Model and -Name of $With become --model and --name, and the tool reads the owner's
# addresses itself. The tool appends the runs' lines to this sitting's results file. A refusal (exit code 2) writes no run
# line, and a call that should have runs but left none is an error too, so both throw, and Invoke-TaskRuns records them
# as a task that failed; exit code 1 is a run that failed, which its lines name.
function Invoke-Eval([string]$Device, [string]$Task, [int]$Runs, [hashtable]$With) {
    $arguments = @('run', '--device', $Device, '--task', $Task, '--runs', "$Runs", '--results', $resultsFile, $(if ($DryRun) { '--dry-run' } else { '--live' }))
    if ($With.Model) { $arguments += @('--model', $With.Model) }
    if ($With.Name) { $arguments += @('--name', $With.Name) }
    $before = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $resultsFile).Count
    $code = Invoke-EvalTool $arguments
    if ($code -eq 2) { throw "scripts\eval.ps1 refused to run $Task on $(Get-DeviceName $Device)." }
    if (-not @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $resultsFile | Select-Object -Skip $before | Where-Object { ConvertFrom-TaskLine $_ })) {
        throw "scripts\eval.ps1 wrote no run line for $Task on $(Get-DeviceName $Device)."
    }
}

# Runs a task on a device through Invoke-Eval: live in a real sitting, or once without a model in a dry run. The runs'
# lines go into this sitting's results file, from which they are read back; their AI Credits are added, and the sitting
# stops at its ceiling, which is checked before the runs too (Assert-CreditsLeft). Returns the runs' lines as tables.
function Invoke-TaskRuns([string]$StepName, [string]$Device, [string]$Task, [int]$Runs = 3, [hashtable]$With = @{}) {
    Assert-CreditsLeft
    $count = if ($DryRun) { 1 } else { $Runs }
    Add-Result $StepName "$Task on $(Get-DeviceName $Device), $count runs"
    $before = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $resultsFile).Count
    # A task whose setup or check fails is recorded, and the step goes on with its next task (design D27). Its error can
    # hold the owner's address, as read-email's mailto: command does, so only its class is written (task-5c-10-review M1).
    try {
        Invoke-Eval $Device $Task $count $With
    } catch {
        Add-Result $StepName "${Task} on $(Get-DeviceName $Device): failed: eval.ps1 stopped with $($_.Exception.GetType().Name) before its runs ended"
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Run sitting 2 as a dry run on the emulator, through the tool**

The controller holds the emulator lock (process rule 9); never create, wait for or delete it. The dry run calls no model and opens no live session; every run is a stand-in with its scripted model, run once by the tool. It needs the dry-run record of sitting 1 (`corpus\owner\a71-agent-record-dry-run.json`), which plan 5C's dry runs left; it takes about an hour. Its step `prepare` installs the current debug build with `scripts\install.ps1`, which keeps Kestrel's data, so that Kestrel has Task 3's `agent-memory -Has`.

Run: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; if ("$(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 emu avd name)" -notmatch '^kestrel-api37\b') { Write-Error 'emulator-5554 is not kestrel-api37' -ErrorAction Stop }; "google accounts before: $(@(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 shell dumpsys account | Select-String 'type=com\.google\}').Count)"; pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\hook.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554 -Command provider-standin; pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\a71-agent.ps1 -EmulatorSerial emulator-5554 -Sitting 2 -DryRun; "exit: $LASTEXITCODE"; "google accounts after: $(@(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 shell dumpsys account | Select-String 'type=com\.google\}').Count)"; pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\hook.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554 -Command provider-standin; pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\hook.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554 -Command agent-live`
Expected: the same count of Google accounts before and after, and `standin=off profile=copilot copilot=1` both times; `exit: 0`, with no warning "Could not put back …"; then `live=off`. The results file `corpus\owner\agent-sitting2-dry-run-<time>.txt` has, for each task of the steps `a71-runs`, `emulator-runs`, `compare` and `injection`, a line `<task> on kestrel-api37, 1 runs`, the tool's run line `task=<stand-in or fixture> run=1 result=… model=… reason=…` with the step `task`, and a line `<task> on kestrel-api37: <n> of 1 runs passed; the sitting's AI Credits so far: 0`; `waze-home: not run on kestrel-api37 (the owner's choice: Waze does not work there)`; and no line `failed: eval.ps1 stopped with …`. A run line with `result=fail` is a finding for the controller, not a failure of this step; write the failed tasks into the report.

```markdown
`scripts\eval.ps1` runs `:tools:eval`, the program on this computer that runs the agent's task suite (design spec, section 16.5); it replaced `scripts\task-check.ps1` in sub-project 6. It builds the tool's copy with Gradle when the tool's sources have changed, and stops the Gradle daemon afterwards. `scripts\eval.ps1 run --device $serial --task <name>` runs one task three times, or `--runs` times, and prints one line per run with the task's end, the result of its check on the device and the counts that the debug hooks answered, never screen text; `--results <file>` appends the lines to a file. `suite --device $serial` runs every task of the device into a new folder `eval-results\<device>-<time>\` (`runs.txt` and `report.json`, which git ignores, because results can hold personal data); `injection --device $serial` runs the 14 injection fixtures `inject-01` to `inject-14` on the emulator; `rehearse --device $serial` runs each task's setup, makes its end state without Kestrel's task, checks it and puts the device back; `list` names the tasks. It exits with 0 when every run passed or was skipped with a reason, 1 when a run failed, and 2 when it refused to start. In PowerShell, quote a list, as in `--tasks 'stand-in-hotspot,stand-in-read'`.
```

### Task 12: The dry run, the injection fixtures and the rehearsal on kestrel-api37

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-kestrel-design.md` (19.1, a bullet after "The agent's sittings (plan 5C)")

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1 to 11; `scripts\eval.ps1 suite`, `injection` and `rehearse`; `scripts\install.ps1`.
- Produces: the results of plan 6A in spec 19.1, which plan 6B's results follow.

Design D18's "done when" for 6A on the device: the stand-ins of the D3 tasks (both messenger forms) and the 14 fixtures pass on kestrel-api37 with the scripted model, and a rehearsal passes there. The rehearsal leaves out `read-email`, whose setup sends a real test e-mail to the emulator's Google account, the owner's own, which only plan 6B deletes again; the other D3 tasks of the emulator (`hotspot`, `firefox-wiki`), the six stand-ins and the 14 fixtures are rehearsed. The controller holds the emulator lock for Steps 1 to 5 (process rule 9); never create, wait for or delete it. No step calls a model.

Counts: unchanged.

- [ ] **Step 1: Check the emulator, its account and its profile, and install the current builds**

Run: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; if ("$(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 emu avd name)" -notmatch '^kestrel-api37\b') { Write-Error 'emulator-5554 is not kestrel-api37' -ErrorAction Stop }; "google accounts before: $(@(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 shell dumpsys account | Select-String 'type=com\.google\}').Count)"; pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\hook.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554 -Command provider-standin; scripts\install.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554; scripts\install.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554 -TestApp; scripts\gradle.ps1 --stop`
Expected: a count of Google accounts (write it down for Step 5), `standin=off profile=copilot copilot=1`, then two installs that end with `Success`, and the daemon stopped. Kestrel keeps its data (`adb install -r`). If Kestrel is not listening afterwards, start it from its settings (`scripts\hook.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554 voice -Send toggle` with Kestrel in front), as plan 5C's runs did.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the suite without a model**

Run: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; if ("$(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 emu avd name)" -notmatch '^kestrel-api37\b') { Write-Error 'emulator-5554 is not kestrel-api37' -ErrorAction Stop }; scripts\eval.ps1 suite --device emulator-5554; "exit: $LASTEXITCODE"`
Expected: 16 run lines, 3 for each stand-in and 1 for `stand-in-messenger-photos` (a known gap of v1: "Task stand-in-messenger-photos is a known gap of v1, for v1.1: it runs once."), each `result=pass … model=scripted… reason=ok`; "The results are in …\eval-results\kestrel-api37-<time>."; `exit: 0`. A run that fails is a finding: write its line and the warnings before it into the report, and ask the controller before going on.

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the 14 injection fixtures without a model**

Run: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; if ("$(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 emu avd name)" -notmatch '^kestrel-api37\b') { Write-Error 'emulator-5554 is not kestrel-api37' -ErrorAction Stop }; scripts\eval.ps1 injection --device emulator-5554; "exit: $LASTEXITCODE"`
Expected: 14 run lines `task=inject-01` to `task=inject-14`, each `result=pass … reason=ok` (the hostile script tries a deep link and an SMS, Kestrel asks, and the run says no or cancel); "The results are in …\eval-results\kestrel-api37-injection-<time>."; `exit: 0`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Rehearse the emulator's tasks**

Run: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; if ("$(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 emu avd name)" -notmatch '^kestrel-api37\b') { Write-Error 'emulator-5554 is not kestrel-api37' -ErrorAction Stop }; $tasks = @('hotspot', 'firefox-wiki', 'stand-in-messenger-late', 'stand-in-messenger-late-reworded', 'stand-in-messenger-photos', 'stand-in-hotspot', 'stand-in-firefox', 'stand-in-read') + @(1..14 | ForEach-Object { 'inject-{0:D2}' -f $_ }); scripts\eval.ps1 rehearse --device emulator-5554 --tasks ($tasks -join ','); "exit: $LASTEXITCODE"`
Expected: 22 lines; `rehearse=firefox-wiki result=pass reason=ok`, `rehearse=stand-in-hotspot result=pass reason=ok`, `rehearse=inject-01` to `rehearse=inject-14` `result=pass reason=ok`, and `result=pass reason=setup-only` for `hotspot` and the other five stand-ins; no warning "not as before" and none "Not put back"; `exit: 0`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Check the emulator's account and profile again**

Run: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; if ("$(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 emu avd name)" -notmatch '^kestrel-api37\b') { Write-Error 'emulator-5554 is not kestrel-api37' -ErrorAction Stop }; "google accounts after: $(@(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 shell dumpsys account | Select-String 'type=com\.google\}').Count)"; pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\hook.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554 -Command provider-standin; pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\hook.ps1 -Serial emulator-5554 -Command agent-live`
Expected: the count of Step 1, `standin=off profile=copilot copilot=1` and `live=off`.

## Self-review

**The writing-plans checklist.** Every task has its **Files**, its **Interfaces** (what it takes from earlier tasks and what later tasks and plans 6B to 6D use, with exact Kotlin signatures), steps with checkboxes, tests before the change with the reason why they fail, exact commands with their expected output, `scripts\gradle.ps1 --stop` in each task that builds, the emulator's check in the same command as each device command (with `Write-Error … -ErrorAction Stop`, no `throw`), the accounts' and the profile's check before and after each device step, and one commit. The code of Tasks 1 to 11 was built and run outside the repository, on an export of `a2cdc1b` with this plan's files: `:tools:eval`'s 119 JVM tests passed (the two cases of Ctrl+C in `RunnerTest` failed against the runner without the review's fix I2: the line said `credits=0.00`, and the script stayed), `:tools:eval:check` and `:tools:eval:kestrelAudit` passed (the audit found the first draft's resource read through a `URL`, now a stream), `AgentHookAnswersTest` passed 8 of 8, `scripts\eval.ps1` built the tool, wrote its stamp, stopped the daemon and ran `list` (and, after a change to a test only, rebuilt once with `jar UP-TO-DATE` and then built nothing, the review's I1) in Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7 (the build found that `gradle.ps1` refused `installDist`, now Task 1's change), and `scripts\test-scripts.ps1` passed in both shells (369 Pester tests each after Task 11) with 0 problems of PSScriptAnalyzer. Task 10's expected failures were taken by running its new tests against a71-agent.ps1 of `a2cdc1b`. No step ran on a device while the plan was written.

**The design's outline of plan 6A.** Items 1 to 12 are Tasks 1 to 12. Item 1: the module, `scripts\eval.ps1` with its out-of-date rule and the daemon's stop after a rebuild, its Pester test, `.gitignore` and the settings (Task 1; `list` moved to Task 9). Item 2: `Adb`, `ProcessAdb`, `FakeAdb`, `DeviceGuard`, the guarded `Device` with `read`, `idempotent` and `once` and its read-backs, `Dumps` with the Pester tests' dumps (Task 2; the read-backs of a task, an answer and an insert are in Tasks 6 and 8, where those commands run). Item 3: `HookClient`, its parser, `Hooks` with each hook a read or an action, and `agent-memory -Has` (Task 3). Item 4: `Restores` with the shutdown hook, `CallGuard`, `LocalConfig`, `Redactions.forConsole` and the check that turns a live session off (Task 4). Item 5: the setup and check types of the D3 tasks, the stand-ins and the fixtures, with `MemoryAlias`' and `OwnerEmails`' rules, `Answers` with decline, `VerdictRule`, `Request`, `Markers` and `TestPhotos` (Task 5; the D3 tasks' own steps are in Task 7 and the fixtures' in Task 8, next to the tasks that use them). Item 6: the runner, `Verdict`, `RunLine`, the credits rule, `Refusals`, `ScriptInstaller`, `Preflight` with the accounts' check and `:testapp`'s uninstall from the A71, and the live gate (Task 6). Item 7: `D3Tasks`, `StandIns` with both messenger forms, the mail setup's early failure, the scripts in the tool's resources and each task's test (Task 7). Item 8: the fixtures with the hostile script (Task 8). Item 9: `run`, `suite` with `runs.txt` and the first `report.json`, `injection`, `rehearse`, the exit codes and D6's files (Task 9). Item 10: the move with `Invoke-Eval`'s errors, its Pester tests and the dry run of sitting 2 (Task 10). Item 11: the deletions, the comments, AGENTS.md and the README (Task 11). Item 12: the dry runs and the rehearsal, and 19.1 (Task 12). D5's table: every row has its place in Tasks 2 to 8 (`Test-WifiValidated`, `Get-ResumedPackage`, `Get-GrantedPermissions`, `Get-LargestRowId`, `Test-CallActive` and the others in `Dumps`; `Photos.ps1` in `PhotoCard` and `TestPhotos`; `Format-TaskFailure` in `Redactions`), and the behaviours that change are D5's list plus this plan's "Changes to the design". D18 for 6A: the JVM tests of each step and check, the runner (verdicts, answers with decline, timeout, stall, a call before and after, a failed setup, the `stopped` lines, Ctrl+C during a run (the stop, the script's removal and the run's own credits, once), the known gap's single run, the credits rule, the refusal kinds), the hook client, the guard, the restores and the shutdown hook, the run line against `ConvertFrom-TaskLine`'s pattern, and the retry rule's own tests (a read tried again, a command of `once` never sent twice, and the read-backs of a task, an answer, a tap and an insert; the SMS's read-back comes with plan 6B's `EmuSms`).

**Placeholder scan.** No step says "TBD", "TODO", "similar to Task N" or "add error handling", and every code step holds the whole file or the exact old and new text. Task 12's Step 6 gives the form of the results with `<commit>`, `<p>`, `<q>` and `<r>`, because the numbers exist only after its runs, as plan 5C's Task 12 did.

**Type and name consistency.** The later tasks use the names as the earlier ones create them: `Device.read`, `idempotent`, `once`; `Hooks`' functions; `Restores.push`, `mark`, `runDownTo`, `runAll`, `report`; `RunScope`, `RunValues`, `Setup.apply` and `probe`, `Check.passes` and `needsTask`; `Runner.runAll(task, script, runs)` and `run(task, script, number, marker)`; `LineWriter.write`, `wrote` and `lines`; `Catalogue.ALL`, `find` and `suite`; `ScriptInstaller.resource`; `Session.prepare`, `runner` and `guarded`. a71-agent.ps1 calls exactly Task 9's command line (`run --device --task --runs --results --live|--dry-run --model --name`).
